These results indicate that centralizing memory management in the agent-system kernel, rather than leaving retrieval and privacy enforcement to individual agents, delivers most of the personalization benefit of unconstrained context at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [190] [Beyond Surface Imitation: Contrastive Modeling for Reasoning Path Alignment in Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10177)
*Mingbo Yang,Wenqiang Wang,Zhaolu Kang,Peng Chen,Yannan Chen,Sunshang Wang,Yan Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的对比式自优化上下文学习框架，通过对比次优答案与更优答案、显式建模推理路径，并结合响应条件检索和轻量级质量控制器，引导模型超越表层模仿。实验表明，该方法在三类多模态任务上稳定提升性能，尤其在视觉问答（VQA）上效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态上下文学习方法主要依赖对示例表面的模仿，难以使模型学习与当前多模态输入相匹配的推理路径。在复杂任务中，这一问题更加突出，限制了MLLM性能的进一步提升。因此，需要一种能够显式呈现答案改进过程，并根据当前回答动态选择相关示例的方法。

Method: 1. 对比示例建模：在相同输入下同时提供次优回答、更优回答以及解释如何从前者改进到后者的推理路径，使目标推理过程更加明确。2. 响应条件检索：根据模型当前回答检索推理路径更相关的示例，以提高后续优化的针对性。3. 轻量级对齐控制器：预测当前回答质量，并判断是否需要继续进行自我优化。整体框架结合了对比学习式示例组织与MLLM的自我细化能力。

Result: 在三类多模态任务上的实验显示，该框架能够稳定提升MLLM性能；其中在视觉问答（VQA）任务上的改进最为明显。摘要未提供具体数据集、基线或数值增益。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模“次优回答—更优回答—推理路径”的对比关系，并结合响应条件示例检索和自适应细化控制，MLLM可以减少对表层示例模仿的依赖，更好地学习与当前输入相关的推理过程，从而提升多模态任务表现。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is widely used in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and achieves strong performance across a wide range of multimodal tasks. However, existing multimodal ICL methods often rely on surface level imitation of in-context demonstrations, making it difficult for MLLMs to align their responses with the reasoning path required by the given multimodal input. This limitation becomes more pronounced in complex multimodal tasks, thereby restricting further improvements in MLLM performance. To address this issue, we propose a new multimodal ICL framework that combines contrastive demonstration modeling with the self-refinement capability of MLLMs. Specifically, our framework reformulates each demonstration by explicitly contrasting a suboptimal response with a better response under the same input, together with a reasoning path that reveals how the response should be refined. This contrastive formulation makes the reasoning path toward the desired response more explicit and guides the MLLM beyond superficial imitation. Furthermore, because effective refinement depends on the current response, we introduce a response-conditioned retrieval mechanism to select demonstrations whose reasoning paths are more relevant to the current response. In addition, we use a lightweight alignment controller to predict response quality and determine whether further refinement is needed. Experiments on three types of multimodal tasks show that the proposed framework consistently improves MLLM performance, with particularly notable gains on visual question answering (VQA).

</details>


### [191] [Why Sample What You Can Enumerate? Exact Policy Optimization for Genomic Tool Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10221)
*Haoyue Liu,Xiaoyu Ma,Ye Chen,Zhichao Wang,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，在专业科学问答中，标准GRPO对工具子集的采样式优化存在结构性缺陷：由于工具子集空间虽可枚举且规模有限，GRPO仍只采样少量rollout，导致策略收敛后奖励碰撞、组归一化优势消失。作者提出FGPO，通过遍历完整工具子集并预计算问题—子集奖励表，实现精确动作期望优化和无冻结推理器在线调用训练。实验显示，FGPO在5个冻结推理器和3个基因组学基准的15个设置中均优于GRPO，平均提升6.75分，最高14.20分；同时降低奖励评估成本和工具调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有“冻结推理器+强化学习”方法通常使用GRPO从工具子集中采样动作并依据少量rollout奖励更新策略。但在专业科学场景中，工具能力种类有限，完整工具子集空间虽然组合化，却足够小，可以被完整枚举。此时采样式估计不仅没有利用可枚举结构，还会随着策略变得集中而反复采样相同工具子集，使奖励重复、组归一化优势趋近于零，训练信号恶化。基因组学实验中，无奖励信号的问题比例从均匀参考策略下的0.2%升至GRPO训练后的20.8%，因此需要一种适配小型可枚举动作空间的优化方法。

Method: 提出Full-Group Policy Optimization（FGPO）。第一，针对每个问题对所有工具子集进行评分，直接计算完整动作空间上的精确动作期望，使每次更新都利用全部候选动作。第二，预先计算并缓存每个“问题—工具子集”组合的奖励，形成穷举奖励表，从而在训练循环中移除冻结推理器调用。该方法本质上将在线、低覆盖率的采样式奖励估计，转化为完整动作空间上的精确期望优化与离线奖励查表。

Result: 在5个冻结推理器、3个基因组学基准构成的15个实验设置中，FGPO全部优于GRPO，平均提升6.75分，最高提升14.20分。与标准按需调用的GRPO训练计划相比，FGPO所需的冻结推理器奖励评估次数仅为其约1/2.4，即GRPO需要约2.4倍评估成本。在GenomeQA上，FGPO还将每道题的工具调用数从2.36降至1.40。实验同时验证了GRPO奖励信号退化现象：无奖励信号比例由0.2%升至20.8%。

Conclusion: 当专业科学任务的工具子集空间规模有限且可完整枚举时，继续采用基于少量采样rollout的GRPO并不匹配问题结构，策略收敛还可能进一步削弱训练信号。FGPO通过完整遍历动作空间和奖励预计算，获得更稳定、精确且成本更低的训练过程，并在多个冻结推理器和基因组学基准上显著优于GRPO。该结论支持针对动作空间结构设计强化学习算法，而非直接套用通用采样式方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning over a frozen reasoner has become a common recipe for teaching a policy which external tools to invoke. We show that this recipe becomes structurally mismatched in specialist scientific settings where the complete tool-subset space is enumerable. There, a small set of recurring computational capabilities covers the domain, so the space of tool subsets is combinatorial yet small enough to enumerate, and GRPO still estimates an action expectation from a handful of sampled rollouts. Worse, the approximation degrades as training succeeds: as the policy concentrates on preferred subsets it resamples them, sampled rewards collide, and the group-normalized advantage vanishes. On genomic reasoning the fraction of questions yielding no reward signal rises from 0.2% under a uniform reference policy to 20.8% after GRPO training. As a remedy, we introduce FGPO (Full-Group Policy Optimization), which (1) scores every tool subset and optimizes the exact action expectation, so each update sees the complete action space, and (2) precomputes the reward of each question--subset pair into an exhaustive table, removing frozen-reasoner calls from the training loop entirely. Across five frozen reasoners and three genomic benchmarks, FGPO outperforms GRPO in all 15 settings by 6.75 points on average and up to 14.20, while a standard on-demand GRPO schedule would require 2.4 times as many frozen-reasoner reward evaluations and, on GenomeQA, FGPO cuts invoked tools per question from 2.36 to 1.40.

</details>


### [192] [What Should an Agent Forget? Separating What Is Stored from What Is Used](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10263)
*Yuhang Li,Yuchen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RD-Forget，一个无需额外训练的记忆管理框架，将“长期保存经验”与“回答时使用哪些信息”分离。它保留完整历史档案，同时根据当前问题构建条件化记忆视图，以抑制过时事实、恢复历史信息，并支持多跳推理。在多类记忆与推理任务上的实验表明，查询相关证据构建和过时信息控制共同决定回答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化语言智能体需要跨时间保存经验，但事实可能更新或被替代。若直接使用全部历史，过时信息会干扰当前状态问题；若永久删除旧信息，又会损害历史查询。因此，需要一种既保留完整历史、又能按问题控制信息影响的记忆机制。

Method: RD-Forget采用训练无关的分离式记忆架构：保留源档案以存储全部观察结果，并在回答时生成查询条件化的记忆视图。冻结语言模型充当整理器，提取相关证据、将事实归入语义槽位，并保留多跳推理所需的关系。对于同一槽位的新旧值，通过替代关系抑制过时值在当前状态上下文中的影响；对于历史意图，则重新开放早期证据。框架进一步利用率失真目标，在给定记忆预算下构建回答时视图。

Result: 实验覆盖对话记忆、知识更新、事实整合、长上下文推理和个性化等场景，并使用统一回答流程。结果显示，准确回答依赖于查询相关证据的构建以及对过时候选信息的控制。缺少遗忘机制或查询条件化的配置得分下降最明显；语义槽位分组、历史信息访问和关系保留分别发挥互补作用。

Conclusion: 保留完整历史并在回答时选择性控制其使用，是应对事实变化和未来历史性问题的可行方案。该研究表明，记忆系统不应简单地永久删除旧信息，而应区分存储层与使用层，并根据用户意图动态调整证据可见性。

Abstract: Persistent language agents need stored experience to remain available across time, while each answer requires evidence suited to a particular question. A superseded fact can mislead a current-state answer and still be essential for a historical query. We present RD-Forget, a training-free framework that separates what an agent stores from what it uses. A retained source archive preserves observations, and a query-conditioned memory view controls their influence on the current answer. A frozen language-model curator extracts relevant evidence, groups facts into semantic slots, and preserves the relations needed for multi-hop reasoning. Same-slot replacement links suppress superseded values in current-state contexts, while intent-aware retrieval makes earlier evidence eligible again. A rate-distortion formulation guides construction of the answer-time view within a memory budget. Experiments span conversational memory, knowledge updating, fact consolidation, long-context reasoning, and personalization under a shared answering pipeline. The results associate accurate answers with both query-relevant evidence construction and control over obsolete alternatives. Configurations without forgetting or query conditioning have the largest score deficits, while slot grouping, historical access, and relation preservation contribute complementary functions. Retaining history while selectively controlling its use offers a practical way to accommodate changing facts and future questions.

</details>


### [193] [TRACE: Training Reasoning Agents for Causal Exploration with Synthesized Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10315)
*Rui Sun,Zhan Shi,Bing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模拟器生成可验证奖励的强化学习方法，用于训练语言模型进行复杂诊断推理。作者在数字广告诊断环境 TRACE 中验证该方法：模型需要通过 Python 和 SQL 分析噪声、混杂且分布式的证据，同时识别根因及受影响细分群体。实验显示，监督微调结合强化学习显著提升性能，RL 后 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 的 FullAttr@1 达到 0.757，超过所评估的提示式基线，并减少工具调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学和代码任务通常具有廉价、客观且可自动检查的答案，因此适合采用带可验证奖励的强化学习。相比之下，复杂数据中的诊断推理往往需要昂贵的专家调查，真实根因可能存在歧义，导致难以获得大规模、可靠的训练奖励。本文的核心动机是通过受控模拟器预先采样干预并生成对应观测，使隐藏干预天然充当 oracle 标签，从而把原本难以验证的诊断任务转化为可规模化验证和训练的问题。

Method: 作者构建 TRACE 数字广告诊断环境，包含 12 类根因和细粒度的受影响分群归因。每个回合先采样一个干预，将其注入受控模拟器，再生成相应观测；智能体使用 Python 和 SQL 调查带噪声、混杂性和分布式的证据，并同时预测根因及适用时的受影响细分群体。训练流程包括监督微调和基于模拟器隐藏干预标签生成的强化学习奖励。评估指标为 FullAttr@1，并比较多个提示式基线和不同规模模型。

Result: 在包含 235 个回合的留出测试集上，最强提示式基线 Claude Opus 5 的 FullAttr@1 为 0.686。Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 经监督微调后从 0.159 提升至 0.637，进一步使用合成奖励进行强化学习后达到 0.757，超过所有被评估的提示式基线，包括前沿闭源模型和提示式 Qwen3.5-122B-A10B。强化学习后的策略还比提示式 35B 基础模型使用更少的工具调用。结果表明，在此类任务中，可扩展且客观的训练信号可能比单纯扩大模型规模更关键。

Conclusion: 本文证明，基于模拟的验证机制能够为原本存在后验歧义、难以获得专家标签的诊断推理任务提供客观奖励，使其适合规模化强化学习。TRACE 实验支持这样一个结论：当诊断环境能够忠实地产生可验证观测和标签时，较小模型通过监督微调与强化学习也可能超过更大模型或闭源模型。该方法的推广价值取决于模拟器的真实性、干预覆盖范围以及模拟分布与真实任务之间的匹配程度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has advanced language-model reasoning in domains such as mathematics and code, where objective answers are inexpensive to check. Diagnostic reasoning over complex data lacks this advantage: establishing the true cause of an anomaly often requires costly expert investigation and may remain ambiguous after the fact. We ask whether this asymmetry of verification can instead be engineered. We sample an intervention, inject it into a controlled simulator, and generate the observations it would produce. The hidden intervention provides an oracle label and objective reward, while the agent must still investigate noisy, confounded, and distributed evidence.
  We instantiate this approach in TRACE, a digital-advertising diagnostic environment with 12 root causes and fine-grained segment attribution. Agents investigate each episode using Python and SQL and must identify both the root cause and, when applicable, the affected segment assignment. On a held-out 235-episode test set, the strongest prompted baseline, Claude Opus 5, reaches 0.686 FullAttr@1. Supervised fine-tuning raises Qwen3.5-35B-A3B from 0.159 to 0.637, and subsequent RL with synthesized rewards reaches 0.757, outperforming all evaluated prompted baselines, including frontier closed-source models and a prompted Qwen3.5-122B-A10B model. The resulting policy also uses substantially fewer tool calls than the prompted 35B base. These results provide evidence that access to a scalable, objective training signal can be a more important constraint than model scale alone. More broadly, simulation-based verification can make otherwise ambiguous diagnostic reasoning tasks amenable to scalable reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [194] [From Symbolic Perception to Logical Deduction: A Framework for Guiding Language Models in Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10335)
*Weichen Dai,Rafael Medeiros Cabral,Ziyi Shou,Yan Cao,Xin Shen,Dongcai Lu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向平面几何题的模块化推理框架：用几何视觉解析器将图形转为符号表示，再由符号求解器执行形式化推导，使纯大语言模型在复杂几何任务上达到接近先进多模态模型的性能，同时提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面几何推理同时依赖图形理解与数学演绎，对人工智能系统构成挑战。现有大型多模态模型虽然能够直接处理视觉—语言输入，但通常计算成本高、推理过程不透明，并可能产生幻觉。因此，本文旨在探索一种更高效、可解释的纯大语言模型方案，将视觉解析与符号推理显式分离，并建立具有新颖性和较高难度的评测基准。

Method: 框架由两个核心模块组成：1）几何视觉解析器，将几何图形及其视觉信息转换为结构化、符号化表示；2）符号求解器，在该表示基础上执行形式化几何推导。作者还从2025年中国中考试题中整理高难度平面几何题，构建评测基准，用于检验模型的深层演绎能力，并将该方法与先进大型多模态模型进行比较。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在复杂平面几何问题上的表现可与Gemini 2.5 Pro相当，同时能够生成更清晰、更符合人类解题方式的解答。模块化设计还有助于减少幻觉，并增强推理过程的可解释性。摘要未提供具体数据集规模、准确率、基线数量或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 将几何图形解析和形式化符号求解结合起来，可以使纯大语言模型有效处理复杂平面几何任务，并在保持竞争性性能的同时改善计算效率、可靠性和可解释性。该工作说明，显式引入专门模块可能是替代端到端多模态推理的一条有效路径。

Abstract: Plane geometry remains a significant challenge in AI, requiring the integration of visual perception and mathematical reasoning. While Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) naturally handle visuo-linguistic inputs, they are often computationally intensive and opaque. We demonstrate that a pure Large Language Model (LLM), when equipped with specialized modules, can rival state-of-the-art LMMs on complex geometry problems. Our framework integrates a Geometric Vision Parser, which translates diagrams into symbolic form, with a Symbolic Solver that performs formal deductions, thereby mitigating hallucinations and promoting interpretable reasoning. To enable rigorous evaluation, we curate a benchmark of challenging problems from the 2025 Chinese Zhongkao examinations, ensuring data novelty and testing deeper deductive skills. Experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves performance comparable to Gemini 2.5 Pro while delivering clearer, human-like solutions.

</details>


### [195] [Cyber-Financial Contagion: Modeling the Propagation of an AI Vendor Compromise Through the Banking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10350)
*Alex Leytes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究人工智能供应商的网络安全事件如何通过运营、信息和金融关联，在银行体系中形成级联冲击，并提出传播仿真模型CFC-Prop与预警模型CFC-GNN。基于合成数据的实验表明，模型能够复现重尾损失和对补丁延迟的敏感性，预警模型取得AUROC 0.82、AUPRC 0.60，说明AI供应商集中度可能成为金融稳定风险的重要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行业越来越依赖少数共享AI供应商，这些供应商同时支持欺诈筛查、信贷决策、反洗钱分流、客户分析和内部决策。一家供应商被攻陷后，风险可能经由服务依赖、信息流和银行间金融敞口扩散，最终表现为类似传统银行危机的系统性损失。现有研究较少将AI供应商依赖与银行间网络、客户账户及风险清算过程统一建模，因此本文试图填补这一空白，并为监管部门提供可量化的风险评估工具。

Method: 构建包含AI供应商、金融机构、银行间敞口和客户账户的四层异质网络；提出CFC-Prop随机流行病—清算模型，用于模拟供应商入侵、风险传播、运营影响和金融损失之间的级联过程。在此基础上，利用供应商侧事件遥测数据与图结构训练CFC-GNN早期预警模型，以识别潜在高级联风险供应商。实验使用包含60家供应商、220家银行、约2500条供应商—银行服务边和1400条银行间敞口的合成数据，并与四种基线比较。

Result: CFC-Prop能够复现与既有网络安全—金融风险证据一致的重尾损失分布，并显示系统损失对补丁修复延迟具有显著敏感性。CFC-GNN在四种基线模型中达到AUROC 0.82、AUPRC 0.60，同时保持受控的校准误差，说明其在识别高级联风险供应商方面具有一定有效性。作者还发布了代码、合成数据和可复现实验脚本。

Conclusion: AI供应商的网络集中度可能是金融稳定性中的一阶风险因素。供应商层面的安全事件并非局部技术故障，而可能通过多层依赖关系演变为跨机构、跨市场的系统性冲击。本文提供了一个结合传播建模与机器学习预警的量化框架，可辅助监管者评估供应商集中风险、补丁延迟影响和潜在级联损失。但由于实验主要依赖合成数据，模型在真实事件数据、参数校准和外部有效性方面仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: The banking system now depends on a small set of shared artificial intelligence vendors for fraud screening, credit decisioning, anti-money-laundering triage, customer analytics, and internal decision support. This paper studies how a compromise inside one of those vendors can propagate along a chain of operational, informational, and financial linkages until it triggers losses that look, from the outside, like a classical banking crisis. We build a four-layer heterogeneous network that couples AI vendors, financial institutions, interbank exposures, and customer accounts, and we propose CFC-Prop, a stochastic epidemic-and-clearing model that runs on that network. On a synthetic dataset with 60 vendors, 220 banks, roughly 2,500 vendor-bank service edges, and 1,400 interbank exposures, CFC-Prop reproduces the heavy-tailed loss distributions and the sharp dependence on patch latency that are consistent with prior cyber-financial evidence. We also train an early-warning model, CFC-GNN, that uses vendor-side incident telemetry and graph structure to flag high-cascade-risk vendors before impact. Across four baselines the proposed model reaches AUROC 0.82 and AUPRC 0.60 while keeping calibration errors bounded. We release the full code, synthetic data, and reproducible scripts. The results argue that cyber concentration among AI vendors is a first-order financial-stability problem and give supervisors a concrete quantitative tool for reasoning about it.

</details>


### [196] [Fortunate Recall: Ontology-Driven Memory Lifecycle Management for Persistent Coherence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10413)
*Ansuman Mullick,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fortunate Recall（FR）记忆生命周期策略层，通过按行为类型管理个人事实的持久化、替换、衰减和检索，以提升时序消歧、冲突处理和事实可靠性。FR-Bank 在 LifecycleBench、LongMemEval 和 BEAM 上优于多个基线，并显著降低混淆性生成；消融实验表明，通用生命周期元数据主要带来正确性提升，而行为本体主要改善校准并降低混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型记忆系统通常将所有个人事实等同处理，导致记忆库无限增长、检索精度下降，并难以处理事实随时间变化、被新事实替代或存在有效期的情况。论文旨在解决记忆生命周期管理问题：判断哪些记忆应长期保留、哪些应被替换、以何种速率衰减，以及如何依据事实类型进行检索。

Method: 提出可组合的 Fortunate Recall（FR）策略层，将个人事实划分为 10+1 类行为本体，并基于 LLM 提取的元数据，以确定性方式实施类别特定的生命周期策略，包括差异化时间衰减、基于槽键的事实替代、事件时间有效性判断和类别感知的检索路由。作者实现了与基础设施无关的 FR-Bank，并在 LifecycleBench（516 个时序消歧问题）、LongMemEval-S 和独立构建的 BEAM 上进行评测；同时开展预注册消融实验，比较完整行为本体与三个通用生命周期原语的差异。

Result: FR-Bank 在 LifecycleBench 上取得 76.9% 通过率，高于 Mem0、A-MEM、Memory-R1 和 MemoryOS 的 61%—70.5%；在 LongMemEval-S 上取得 75.2%，表明生命周期策略未对标准检索造成可测量损失。消融显示，替换行为类型层后正确率变化为 -1.7 个百分点，95% CI 为 [-6.0, +2.7]，说明通用生命周期元数据主要贡献正确性；行为本体则将下游混淆率从 24.2% 降至 12.0%。端到端比较中，FR-Bank 将 Mem0 在已回答问题上的混淆率从 45.1% 降至 22.4%，在全部问题上的混淆率从 32.2% 降至 13.0%，同时正确回答率从 18.6% 提升至 31.2%。在 Kimi K2.5 上结果同样复现；BEAM 上 FR-Bank 正确率为 46.8%，高于 Mem0 的 32.9%，且行为本体的收益主要集中于矛盾消解，并在约七个策略簇附近趋于饱和。

Conclusion: 论文表明，记忆系统的关键不只是增加存储容量或改进检索，而是引入显式、可组合的生命周期管理。通用生命周期元数据能够提升事实处理正确性，行为类型本体则进一步改善校准、降低混淆，尤其有助于矛盾消解。FR 的方法具有基础设施无关性和跨模型、跨基准的迁移性；作者同时发布了行为本体、基准和代码，以支持复现和后续研究。

Abstract: Current LLM memory systems treat all personal facts identically, so stores grow without bound while retrieval precision degrades. The core challenge is lifecycle management: which memories should persist, which should be replaced, and at what rate, conditioned on the behavioral type of each fact. Fortunate Recall (FR) is a composable policy layer that classifies personal facts into a 10+1 behavioral ontology and applies category-specific lifecycle policies (differential temporal decay, slot-key supersession, event-time validity, and category-aware retrieval routing) as deterministic functions over LLM-extracted metadata. FR-Bank, our infrastructure-independent implementation, reaches a 76.9% pass rate on LifecycleBench, a new 516-question temporal-disambiguation benchmark, ahead of Mem0, A-MEM, Memory-R1, and MemoryOS (61% to 70.5%), and 75.2% on the full LongMemEval-S under the canonical Wu et al. judge protocol, so lifecycle policies impose no measurable cost on standard retrieval. A pre-registered ablation locates the gains: replacing the typed layer with three generic lifecycle primitives leaves correctness statistically unchanged (-1.7pp, 95% CI [-6.0, +2.7]), so the generic lifecycle metadata carries the correctness advantage, while the behavioral ontology carries calibration, halving downstream confabulation (12.0% vs 24.2%, p<0.001). End-to-end, FR-Bank cuts confabulation from Mem0's 45.1% to 22.4% over answered queries and from 32.2% to 13.0% over all queries while answering more of them correctly (31.2% vs 18.6%); the ranking replicates on the open-weight Kimi K2.5. The decomposition transfers to BEAM, an independently built benchmark: 46.8% correct vs Mem0's 32.9% over 280 questions, with the ontology's benefit concentrated in contradiction resolution and saturating near seven policy clusters. The ontology, benchmark, and code are released.

</details>


### [197] [ConvMem: Convolutional Memory for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10441)
*Hongming Zhang,Zhaozhen Gu,Fengshuo Bai,Ming Hao,Qingyang Zhang,Yuanyuan Wang,Shiyang Tang,Yanna Wang,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConvMem 是一种无需训练、可高度并行化的长上下文推理框架，将分段文本的层次化摘要过程建模为类似卷积神经网络的层次卷积。它通过可配置步幅、跳跃连接和多核卷积，缩短推理路径、增强证据传播并支持复杂查询分解。实验表明，该方法在 RULER-HotpotQA 和 RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA 上优于训练免基线，同时降低了 RL 方法在分布外任务上的过拟合风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型受固定上下文窗口限制，在超长文本上推理能力不足。MemAgent 等顺序式方法虽然能够通过迭代更新固定大小记忆来扩展有效上下文，但存在推理延迟高、并行性差、需要昂贵强化学习训练以及可能对特定数据集过拟合等问题。因此，论文旨在设计一种无需训练、低延迟、可并行且具有更强分布外泛化能力的长上下文推理方法。

Method: 提出 ConvMem，将长上下文推理重新表述为层次化卷积过程：将带有特定查询提示的 LLM 视为卷积核，对文本片段进行逐层摘要，并将线性推理链缩短为对数深度的树结构。方法包含三项关键设计：1）Configurable Strides（可配置步幅），调节片段处理范围和重叠方式，以提高证据捕获能力；2）Skip Connections（跳跃连接），促进关键信息跨层传播并减轻误差累积；3）Multi-Kernel Convolution（多核卷积），将复杂查询拆分为相互解耦的语义通道。整体流程无需额外训练，并可在文本片段和推理线程两个维度上大规模并行。

Result: 在 RULER-HotpotQA 和 RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA 数据集上的实验显示，ConvMem 优于训练免基线方法。摘要还指出，相比经过强化学习训练的方法，ConvMem 在分布外任务上能够避免对参数化先验的过度依赖及由此产生的过拟合风险。

Conclusion: ConvMem 证明了将长上下文推理建模为层次化卷积是可行的。通过对数级推理路径、跳跃式证据传播、多核语义分解和高度并行执行，该方法在无需训练的前提下兼顾了效率、鲁棒性与泛化能力，为突破 LLM 固定上下文限制提供了新的框架。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities, they often struggle with extremely long contexts due to fixed context limits. To address this, sequential approaches like MemAgent extend the effective context by reading text in segments and iteratively updating a fixed-size memory. However, this sequential paradigm suffers from high latency and requires costly reinforcement learning (RL) training, which can lead to overfitting on specific datasets. To overcome these limitations, we propose ConvMem, a training-free, highly parallelizable framework that reformulates long-context reasoning as a hierarchical convolution. Inspired by CNNs, ConvMem treats an LLM prompted with a specific query as a convolutional kernel. This kernel summarizes text segments hierarchically, shortening the reasoning path from a linear chain into a logarithmic tree. Specifically, ConvMem integrates \textit{Configurable Strides} and \textit{Skip Connections} to ensure robust evidence capture and propagation, while employing \textit{Multi-Kernel Convolution} to decompose complex queries into disentangled semantic channels. This design not only mitigates error accumulation but also enables massive parallelization across both text segments and reasoning threads. Experiments on RULER-HotpotQA and RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA demonstrate that ConvMem outperforms training-free baselines and avoids the risk of overfitting to parametric priors often observed in RL-trained models on out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [198] [JarvisGUI: Towards Cross-Device GUI Agents with Dynamic Task Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10451)
*Zixiang Chen,Yuheng Lu,Zihao Cheng,Zeming Liu,Jizeng Bai,Ziye Huang,Zhiyin Lin,Zihan Li,Yuhang Guo,Yunhong Wang,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JarvisGUI 是一个面向跨设备、跨平台 GUI 工作流的动态基准，用于评估智能体在 Android、Windows 和 Ubuntu 等异构环境中的协同操作能力。该基准将 GUI 任务建模为基于轻量类型系统的输入—输出变换，可自动组合多步骤工作流并动态评测。实验表明，现有开源 GUI 智能体在状态转移感知、跨平台上下文推理和长程依赖管理方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的 GUI 使用通常跨越多个设备与平台，需要传递中间结果、维护共享状态并协调异构环境。然而，现有 GUI 基准主要聚焦于单设备、静态任务，无法充分检验这些能力，因而可能过高估计智能体面向真实应用的准备程度。

Method: 提出 JarvisGUI 动态基准：1）覆盖 Android、Windows 和 Ubuntu 等多种平台；2）将 GUI 任务形式化为基于轻量类型系统的输入—输出变换；3）利用该形式自动组合多步骤、跨设备工作流；4）在统一的虚拟多操作系统环境中动态评估 GUI 智能体表现。

Result: 评测结果显示，当前先进的开源 GUI 智能体难以处理跨设备工作流中的状态转移感知、跨平台上下文推理和长程依赖管理问题。JarvisGUI 揭示了现有单设备基准未能发现的关键能力缺口。

Conclusion: 跨设备、跨平台和动态工作流是衡量 GUI 智能体真实可用性的关键维度。JarvisGUI 为此提供了统一的自动化评测框架，并表明现有开源智能体距离可靠支持现实世界 GUI 工作流仍有明显差距。

Abstract: Real-world GUI usage frequently involves workflows that span multiple devices and platforms, requiring the transfer of intermediate results, maintenance of shared state, and coordination across heterogeneous environments. However, existing GUI benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate agents on single-device, statically defined tasks, thus leaving such cross-device capabilities largely unexamined, resulting in an overly optimistic assessment of agents' readiness for real-world usage. We introduce JarvisGUI, a dynamic benchmark that evaluates GUI agents on cross-device workflows requiring coordinated interaction across heterogeneous platforms, including Android, Windows, and Ubuntu. Specifically, JarvisGUI formulates GUI tasks as input-output transformations under a lightweight type system, which allows us to automatically compose multi-step, cross-device workflows and dynamically evaluate agent performance within a unified framework. By evaluating agents in virtual environments spanning multiple operating systems, JarvisGUI reveals that state-of-the-art open-source GUI agents struggle with the state-transfer awareness, cross-platform contextual reasoning, and long-horizon dependency management required for real-world workflows, exposing a critical capability gap invisible to existing benchmarks.

</details>


### [199] [Less Is Personal: Learning Minimal Sufficient User Profiles for Personalized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08180)
*Minghang Liu,Qiang Qiu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 ENOUGH，一种自适应构建最小充分个性化用户档案的方法：按序选择行为记录，并在信息足够时停止，以较低上下文成本保持检索增强生成的个性化效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化语言模型通常固定拼接数量的用户历史记录，容易引入冗余、有害或无关信息，增加上下文成本并可能削弱输出质量。论文旨在研究“最小充分个性化”：针对每个输入，仅选择足以保留候选记录池所能达到效用的最小有序档案。

Method: 提出 ENOUGH。离线阶段使用有界反事实搜索评估不同档案前缀，联合考虑下游效果增益、用户特异性和 token 成本，并将长时程决策目标蒸馏为带有显式排序与停止监督的多头价值控制器。在线阶段，控制器以轻量决策逐步选择并排序行为记录，必要时输出 STOP；停止后仅调用一次冻结的生成模型。

Result: 在六个个性化任务上的大量实验表明，ENOUGH 相较于强启发式方法和检索增强基线，在效果与效率两个方面均表现更优。它能够在保持个性化效用的同时，减少不必要的上下文记录和 token 成本。摘要未提供具体数据、数据集名称或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 论文证明，个性化上下文不必通过固定长度或尽可能多的历史记录构建。通过联合建模记录排序、效用、用户特异性与停止决策，可以获得自适应长度的最小充分用户档案，在维持生成质量的同时提升推理效率。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented personalization enables large language models to produce more accurate and preference-aligned outputs using relevant records retrieved from user histories. Personalized language models typically prepend a fixed number of retrieved user records, even when additional history is redundant, harmful, or unrelated to a user's distinctive behavior. We study minimal sufficient personalization: constructing the least costly ordered profile for each input while preserving the utility achievable from a retrieved candidate pool. We introduce ENOUGH, a method that iteratively appends behavioral records or emits STOP to construct profiles with adaptive lengths. Offline, bounded counterfactual search evaluates profile prefixes by jointly considering downstream gains, user specificity, and token costs. The resulting long-horizon targets are distilled into a multi-head value controller with explicit ranking and stopping supervision. At inference, the controller selects and orders records through lightweight decisions, and the frozen generator is invoked once after stopping. Extensive experiments on six personalized tasks demonstrate that ENOUGH consistently outperforms strong heuristic and retrieval-augmented baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency, achieving minimal sufficient profiles that preserve personalization utility while reducing unnecessary context costs.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Stability of Fork-Join Systems with Redundancy and Heterogeneous Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09237)
*Chutong Gao,Seyed Iravani,Ohad Perry*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究具有冗余机制和异构服务器的 fork-join 系统在静态与动态容量分配策略下的稳定性。作者刻画最大稳定区域，并给出两类策略达到该区域的充分条件；核心方法是将一般的 (n,k) 系统投影为无冗余的 (k,k) 系统，并利用广义 Schur 凸序建立多维样本路径比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冗余 fork-join 系统能够通过只等待部分任务完成来降低作业完成时间，但冗余任务会消耗异构服务器的服务能力，使系统稳定性分析变得复杂。现有问题的关键在于：在不同容量分配机制下，系统可稳定的最大负载范围是什么，以及何种分配策略能够达到这一理论上限。

Method: 首先定义名义交通强度并刻画最大稳定区域，即存在某个可行策略使系统稳定的交通强度集合。随后分别研究静态和动态容量分配策略：静态策略保持各服务器服务容量不变；动态策略允许在服务器之间反复重新分配固定总容量。技术上，将 (n,k) FJR 系统投影到无冗余的 (k,k) 系统，并构造基于广义 Schur 凸序的多维过程样本路径比较方法，以比较队列状态及其稳定性。

Result: 对于静态分配策略，当最快服务器获得的服务容量不超过总服务容量的 1/k 时，最大稳定区域能够达到。对于动态分配策略，当任意 j=1,...,k-1 时，分配给 j 个最短队列的累计容量不超过总容量的 j/k，最大稳定区域也能够达到。研究同时给出了名义交通强度和最大稳定区域的刻画。

Conclusion: 冗余系统的稳定性不仅取决于总服务能力，还取决于容量在异构服务器和不同长度队列之间的分配方式。适当限制最快服务器或最短队列获得的容量，可以消除冗余机制对稳定区域造成的不利影响，使系统达到最大稳定性能。投影方法与广义 Schur 凸序为分析多维冗余队列系统提供了新的通用工具。

Abstract: We consider the stability problem of fork-join systems with redundancy (FJR) and heterogeneous servers under both static and dynamic capacity-allocation policies. In an $(n,k)$ FJR system, each arriving job is split into $n$ independent tasks, with one task assigned to each of $n$ parallel servers. Once $k \le n$ tasks have been processed, they are joined and the corresponding job departs the system; the remaining $n-k$ unprocessed tasks are then removed and are therefore termed redundant. We first identify the nominal traffic intensity and characterize the maximal stability region, defined as the set of traffic intensities for which there exists an admissible policy that stabilizes the system. We then establish conditions under which this maximal stability region is attained for two classes of policies: static and dynamic. Specifically, we show that for static allocation policies, in which service capacities remain fixed over time, maximality is achieved whenever the fastest server is allocated no more than $1/k$ of the total service capacity. For dynamic allocation policies, in which a fixed total service capacity may be repeatedly reallocated among the servers, we show that maximality is achieved whenever the cumulative capacity allocated to the $j$ shortest queues does not exceed $j/k$ of the total capacity for every $j=1,\ldots,k-1$. Our analysis is based on a projection of the $(n,k)$ FJR system onto a simpler $(k,k)$ system that has no redundancy, together with a novel sample-path comparison argument for multidimensional processes based on the generalized Schur-convex order.

</details>


### [201] [Optimality of Kløve Arrays within the Symmetric Kløve-Mossige Class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09239)
*Lilin Yan,Hongwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明：在指定的、具有连续和共阵的最小冗余对称 Kløve–Mossige 阵类中，任意固定传感器数量下的最优传感器集合都属于 Kløve 阵类。因此，原问题可化为已知的 Kløve 参数搜索；结论覆盖全部传感器数量，其中 2–329 由精确整数证书验证，至少 330 由理论界限证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是回答 Rajamäki 与 Koivunen 提出的结构性问题：在对称 Kløve–Mossige 阵且和共阵连续的条件下，具有最小冗余并最大孔径的最优阵是否必然属于更具体的 Kløve 类。若答案为肯定，则可显著缩小优化搜索空间，并将一般类的优化转化为已有 Kløve 参数上的搜索。

Method: 方法结合理论分析与计算机辅助证明。首先分类生成器与其平移反射之间可能出现的重叠模式；随后对假设位于 Kløve 类之外的最优解建立孔径上界，并将其与经典 Kløve 构造的孔径进行比较，从而证明传感器数量至少为 330 时不存在类外最优解。对于 2 至 329 的剩余规模，使用精确整数证书进行穷举或形式化验证，并检查所有等号情形均对应 Kløve 阵。

Result: 结果表明，在每个固定传感器数量下，所有最大孔径的传感器集合都属于 Kløve 类。理论比较处理了传感器数量至少为 330 的情形，精确整数证书处理了 2–329 的情形；所有达到最优值的等号情形也都与 Kløve 阵一致。

Conclusion: 在所明确限定的对称 Kløve–Mossige 阵类及连续和共阵条件下，优化问题可完全约化为已知的 Kløve 参数搜索。该结论不适用于无约束稀疏阵，也不声称覆盖所有受限加性基。

Abstract: Rajam$\unicode{228}$ki and Koivunen asked whether minimum-redundancy symmetric Kl$\unicode{248}$ve-Mossige arrays with contiguous sum co-arrays are always Kl$\unicode{248}$ve arrays. We give a computer-assisted proof that, at every fixed sensor count, every maximizing sensor set belongs to the Kl$\unicode{248}$ve class. The argument classifies the overlaps between a generator and its shifted reflection, then bounds the aperture of a hypothetical optimizer outside the Kl$\unicode{248}$ve class. Comparing these bounds with a classical Kl$\unicode{248}$ve construction settles all sensor counts at least 330. An exact integer certificate covers the remaining counts from 2 through 329 and matches every equality case to a Kl$\unicode{248}$ve array as a complete set. Consequently, optimization within the full symmetric Kl$\unicode{248}$ve-Mossige class reduces to the previously known search over Kl$\unicode{248}$ve parameters. The theorem concerns this specified class, rather than unrestricted sparse arrays or all restricted additive bases.

</details>


### [202] [On the Sequential Test and Distributed Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09358)
*Earnest Akofor*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究序贯检测中的停止时间，提出简洁定义及其在集中式和分布式检测中的统一应用，并将结果推广至以无环有向图表示的分布式检测网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序贯检测与分布式检测问题通常涉及复杂的停止规则和决策规则推导，缺乏一种简洁且统一的停止时间表述。本文旨在建立清晰的定义和直接的求解流程，以便获得最优决策规则，并刻画最优停止时间的上界及其性质。

Method: 首先给出停止时间的简单定义，并将其嵌入集中式与分布式检测的序贯检验框架。随后建立获取最优决策规则的直接程序，推导最优停止时间的上界，并通过数值实验考察这些上界是否呈现理论上预期的定性特征。最后，将相关理论推广到由无环有向图描述的任意分布式检测网络。

Result: 论文推导了最优停止时间的上界，并通过数值结果表明这些上界具有若干符合预期的定性特征。理论结果同时适用于集中式检测、分布式检测以及基于无环有向图的分布式检测网络。摘要未给出具体数据、误差指标或性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个简洁统一的停止时间定义和最优决策规则构造方法，能够覆盖集中式及广泛的分布式检测场景。所得停止时间上界具有合理的定性表现，并可推广至任意无环有向图网络。

Abstract: We present a simple definition of stopping time and its role in the formulation of sequential tests for both centralized and distributed detection, providing a straightforward procedure for obtaining optimal decision rules. Upper bounds for optimal stopping time are derived and numerically shown to possess certain qualitative features expected of the optimal stopping time. The results are extended to any distributed detection network in the form of an acyclic directed graph.

</details>


### [203] [Degree Sequence Reconstruction from Subgraph Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09397)
*Venkata Gandikota,Arick Grootveld,Haodong Yang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究从顶点独立删除得到的图迹中重构未知图的有序度序列，并分析重构度序列及边数所需的迹复杂度。论文提出两类方法：基于拒绝采样与混合分布估计的方法，以及基于度矩与极值多项式的方法。前者达到 exp(\tilde O(n^{1/3})) 迹复杂度，后者达到 exp(\tilde O(n^{1/2}))；同时证明重构度序列需要且只需 \tilde\Theta(n^{1/2}) 个度矩，并给出边数重构的 O(n^3) 上界与 \Omega(n^2) 下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶点删除轨迹重构是图重构与迹重构中的基础问题。度序列是图的重要结构不变量，恢复它有助于理解顶点删除噪声下可恢复的信息边界。论文进一步关注边数这一更简单的图属性，旨在确定其重构所需的迹数量，并刻画算法效率与信息论复杂度之间的关系。

Method: 论文提出两种度序列重构方法。第一种方法利用拒绝采样，将顶点删除轨迹问题转化为混合分布的估计问题，再结合已有的迹重构结果。第二种方法从轨迹中恢复度矩，并利用极值多项式界分析度矩对度序列的区分能力：证明需要且足够约 \tilde\Theta(n^{1/2}) 个度矩，并据此构造重构算法及次指数时间解码器。对于边数，论文分别建立重构算法的上界和不可避免的下界。

Result: 第一种度序列重构方法的迹复杂度为 exp(\tilde O(n^{1/3}))，但目前尚无次指数时间解码器。基于度矩的方法需要 exp(\tilde O(n^{1/2})) 条迹，并具有次指数时间解码器。度序列可由 \tilde\Theta(n^{1/2}) 个度矩识别，说明该数量级在信息与算法意义上都是紧的。边数重构的迹复杂度介于 \Omega(n^2) 与 O(n^3) 之间。

Conclusion: 论文建立了从顶点删除迹重构图度序列的两条技术路线，并用极值多项式工具精确刻画了所需度矩的数量级。结果表明，较低迹复杂度与高效解码之间存在权衡：拒绝采样方法在迹复杂度上更优，而度矩方法具有更明确的结构解释和次指数时间解码器。边数重构的上下界仍未完全匹配，留下了进一步缩小复杂度间隔的问题。

Abstract: The goal of degree sequence reconstruction is to recover the ordered vector of degrees of an unknown graph from vertex deleted traces, where each vertex is deleted independently with probability $p$. We provide two algorithms for reconstruction; the first uses rejection sampling to reduce the problem to an estimation problem for a mixture distribution. Combined with prior trace reconstruction results, this gives a reconstruction algorithm using $\Exp{\tilde O (n^{1/3})}$ traces, although no sub-exponential time decoder is known. Our other approach involves recovering certain graph invariants, degree moments, that can identify a graphs degree sequence. Extremal polynomial bounds show that $\tilde Θ(n^{1/2})$ degree moments are necessary and sufficient to reconstruct the degree sequence, which leads to an algorithm with $\Exp{\tilde O(n^{1/2})}$ trace complexity. The same polynomial machinery yields a sub-exponential time decoder for the degree sequence from the moments. Additionally, we give an $O(n^{3})$ upper bound and a $Ω(n^2)$ lower bound for the trace complexity of recovering the number of edges.

</details>


### [204] [Characterizing Multi-Cell Pinching-Antenna Transmission: Revealing the Other Side of the Coin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09585)
*Zhiguo Ding*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文研究可“收缩/夹持”天线（pinching antennas）在无线网络中的干扰抑制优势。不同于仅增强用户与自身基站（BS）的信号链路，本文指出：pinching antennas可使各基站以较低发射功率服务用户，从而降低网络干扰，即使用户自身链路质量不变，数据速率仍可能提升。论文基于随机几何理论和分数功率控制，给出解析与数值结果，验证了该干扰抑制能力的显著作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注pinching antennas如何增强用户与其服务基站之间的连接质量，而其对网络干扰的抑制作用尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在揭示一种较不直观但重要的收益：通过降低各基站的服务发射功率，pinching antennas能够减少邻近基站对用户造成的干扰，进而提升系统数据率。

Method: 建立采用pinching antennas的蜂窝网络随机几何模型，并假设基站采用分数功率控制。通过随机几何分析推导相关性能指标，随后结合数值计算评估pinching antennas对发射功率、干扰水平和用户数据率的影响。核心机制是：天线部署或位置调整改善服务链路后，基站无需高功率传输，可实现低功率“低声通信”，从而降低整个网络的干扰。

Result: 解析和数值结果表明，pinching antennas能够显著降低基站发射功率及用户所经历的干扰。因此，即便用户与自身基站之间的链路质量保持不变，用户数据率仍可因干扰减弱而提升。结果验证了pinching antennas在干扰抑制方面具有重要性能收益。

Conclusion: pinching antennas的价值不仅在于增强服务链路，还在于促使基站以更低功率运行，从网络层面抑制干扰。该干扰抑制能力可显著提升用户数据率，说明pinching antennas是改善无线网络频谱效率和整体性能的有效技术。

Abstract: Using pinching antennas to enhance a user's connection to its own base station (BS) is intuitive and has been well investigated in the literature. This letter focuses on a less intuitive advantage of pinching antennas from the interference-suppression perspective. In particular, using pinching antennas ensures that all BSs serve their users with low transmit power, i.e., BSs can ``whisper", rather than ``shout", to their users. As a result, by experiencing less interference, a user's data rate can still be improved with pinching antennas, even if its link quality to its own BS remains unchanged. A stochastic geometric study is carried out in the letter, where BSs employ fractional power control. Analytical and numerical results are presented to reveal the significant impact of this interference-suppression capability offered by pinching antennas.

</details>


### [205] [Fundamental Limits of Joint Target Detection and Parameter Estimation - Characterizing Mixed-State Sensing Limits via Posterior Entropy Volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09667)
*Dazhuan Xu,Nan Wang,Han Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文建立了统一的混合离散—连续状态感知理论：以混合熵体积和互信息刻画后验不确定性收缩，证明最优后验混合熵体积按先验体积乘以2^{-I(Ξ;Y)}收缩，并刻画级联压缩造成的信息损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成感知与通信需要统一的感知理论，同时目标存在模式属于离散变量、物理参数属于连续变量，传统仅适用于单一类型状态的熵或互信息难以同时表达物理尺度、目标数量变化以及通信信息量。因此，论文旨在建立适用于混合离散—连续状态的测度论框架，并统一检测与估计的理论描述。

Method: 将目标状态建模为基于分支参考测度的混合离散—连续状态Ξ；分别定义离散、连续和混合状态的熵数、熵体积与混合熵体积。利用固定支撑测度下均匀分布的最大熵原理解释指数熵尺度的测度含义，并结合混合渐近等分性质、条件典型集和后验概率—体积不等式，推导高概率可靠感知区域的最小一阶后验混合熵体积。进一步利用互信息链式法则分解检测与估计贡献，并分析后验保持级联和任意中间压缩的性能。

Result: 证明了最小可达的一阶后验混合熵体积等于先验混合熵体积乘以2^{-I(Ξ;Y)}，其中I(Ξ;Y)=I(V;Y)+I(X_V;Y|V)。因此，检测与估计在体积域中呈乘法组合，在比特域中呈加法组合；每获得1比特感知信息，后验有效测度减半。后验保持级联能够达到直接推断极限，而任意中间压缩的精确信息损失为I(Ξ;Y|Z)。单目标存在—距离模型的数值实验验证了信息分解、后验熵体积收缩和级联接口损失。

Conclusion: 论文建立了混合状态感知的统一信息论基础，揭示了互信息与后验熵体积之间的定量对应关系：互信息提供与坐标和单位无关的比特度量，熵体积则保留可与工程分辨率尺度比较的物理意义。该框架统一了检测和估计，并为感知链路设计、接口压缩及集成感知通信系统的性能分析提供了理论工具。

Abstract: The development of integrated sensing and communication calls for a unified theoretical foundation for sensing. This paper models target-presence patterns and continuous physical parameters as a mixed discrete-continuous state $Ξ$ on a branched reference measure, and treats posterior entropy volume and joint mutual information as two complementary representations of the same limit. Entropy volume carries physical units, can be compared with engineering scales such as resolution cells, and remains meaningful when the number of active targets varies; mutual information is dimensionless, invariant to coordinates and units, and additive through the chain rule. We define entropy number, entropy volume, and mixed entropy volume for discrete, continuous, and mixed states, respectively. The maximum-entropy principle for the uniform distribution on a support of fixed measure explains the measure-theoretic meaning of the exponential entropy scale, while the main limit follows from a mixed asymptotic equipartition property and a posterior probability-volume inequality through conditional typical sets. For asymptotically reliable high-probability sensing regions, the minimum achievable first-order posterior mixed entropy volume equals the prior mixed entropy volume multiplied by $2^{-I(Ξ;Y)}$, where $I(Ξ;Y)=I(V;Y)+I(X_V;Y\mid V)$. Thus, detection and estimation contributions multiply in the volume domain and add in the bit domain, and one sensing bit halves the posterior effective measure. We further prove that posterior-preserving cascades attain the direct-inference limit, while arbitrary intermediate compression incurs the exact information loss $I(Ξ;Y\mid Z)$. Numerical results for a single-target presence-range model illustrate the information composition, posterior entropy-volume contraction, and cascade-interface loss.

</details>


### [206] [Conditions for Global Optimality in Quantum Arimoto-Blahut Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09731)
*Geng Liu,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究广义 Arimoto–Blahut（AB）算法固定点的全局最优性，建立判别条件并提出可计算的事后最优性证书；结果表明单调下降和数值稳定并不足以保证全局最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AB 算法广泛用于信息论和量子优化，但现有的单调目标下降、收敛或数值稳定性只能说明算法行为良好，不能证明所得固定点是全局最优。论文旨在明确 AB 固定点何时具有全局最优性，厘清 AB 与镜像下降（MD）的关系，并提供无需显式求解全局问题的可计算检验工具。

Method: 在凸可微目标和线性约束下，分析满秩 AB 固定点的更新方向、目标梯度与约束法空间之间的关系，推导全局最优的充要条件。进一步比较 AB 与 MD 的单步更新及共同轨迹，构造基于可行方向导数的事后最优性证书，并给出仅依赖目标函数值的有限差分目标差上界。通过去相位通道与退极化通道的信道相对熵问题进行解析和数值验证，同时用振幅阻尼通道展示反例。

Result: 证明了：满秩 AB 固定点全局最优，当且仅当 AB 更新方向与目标梯度之差属于约束法空间。该条件也刻画 AB 与 MD 单步更新的一致性，但轨迹等价要求该条件沿整个共同轨迹成立，因此 AB 和 MD 可沿不同轨迹到达同一全局最优解。对于去相位—退极化信道相对熵，唯一的满秩 AB 固定点即为全局最小点，尽管两种算法不具备路径等价性。数值实验验证了不同轨迹上的收敛及证书有效性；振幅阻尼例子表明，单调 AB 迭代可能停留在次优固定点，而有限差分证书能够识别其非最优性。

Conclusion: 论文建立了量子 AB 算法全局最优性的一套结构性、可计算判据：固定点性质、AB/MD 更新一致性与路径等价性应严格区分；单调收敛不能替代全局最优性证明。所提出的方向导数和有限差分证书可用于对算法输出进行事后可靠评估，具有理论分析和实际诊断价值。

Abstract: Generalized Arimoto--Blahut (AB) algorithms are widely used in information theory and quantum optimization, but monotonic objective decrease and numerical stabilization do not guarantee global optimality. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the global optimality of full-rank AB fixed points for convex differentiable objectives under linear constraints. An AB fixed point is globally optimal if and only if the AB update direction and the objective gradient differ by an element of the constraint normal space at that point. The same compatibility condition characterizes agreement between individual AB and mirror-descent (MD) updates, whereas pathwise equivalence requires it along the entire common trajectory. Thus, an AB algorithm may follow a trajectory different from MD and still reach the global optimum. We also develop a posteriori optimality certificates based on feasible directional derivatives, including finite-difference upper bounds on the objective gap that require only objective evaluations. For channel relative entropy between dephasing and depolarizing channels, we analytically identify the global minimizer as the unique full-rank AB fixed point, although pathwise equivalence fails. Numerical experiments illustrate convergence of AB and MD along different trajectories and validate the certificates. By contrast, an amplitude-damping example shows that a monotone AB iteration can stabilize at a suboptimal fixed point, whose nonoptimality is detected by the finite-difference certificate. These results provide structural and computable criteria for assessing global optimality in quantum AB algorithms.

</details>


### [207] [Channel Estimation for OFDM via Delay-Doppler Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09782)
*Mingcheng Nie,Hao Chang,Xiaoqi Zhang,Junkai Liu,Wibowo Hardjawana,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向OFDM系统的两阶段信道估计算法：先在时频域进行粗估计，再利用单位变换转至时延-多普勒域，通过阈值法精确估计信道参数，最后反馈形成精细的时频域信道估计。在相同导频配置下，该方法优于传统OFDM信道估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OFDM信道估计方法未充分利用时延-多普勒域信道的稀疏性、结构性及其独特特征，因而在相同导频开销下估计精度和性能仍有限。本文旨在利用DD域信道结构提升信道估计准确率。

Method: 首先，仅保留时频域信道矩阵中的非零元素，推导紧凑的TF域输入输出关系；随后采用线性最小均方误差估计器进行粗略TF域信道估计。将粗估计结果通过酉变换映射到DD域，并发现有效DD域信道矩阵可表示为多个具有不同相位偏移的DD域信道响应的聚合。在此基础上，采用基于阈值的参数估计方法提取DD域信道参数，再利用这些参数重构精细的TF域信道估计。

Result: 数值结果表明，在相同导频配置下，所提方法相较传统OFDM信道估计技术能够获得显著的性能增益，说明其在信道估计精度方面具有优势。摘要未给出具体的误差指标、信噪比范围或增益数值。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合TF域的低复杂度粗估计与DD域结构化参数精估计，有效利用了DD域信道特性，并在不增加导频部署的条件下提升了OFDM信道估计性能。其核心贡献在于紧凑TF域模型、TF-DD域联合估计框架以及基于相位偏移聚合特性的阈值参数估计。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel channel estimation (CE) algorithm for orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) systems that exploits the unique characteristics of the delay-Doppler (DD) domain channel. Specifically, the time-frequency (TF) domain input-output relationship (IOR) is derived in a compact form by focusing solely on the non-zero elements of the TF domain channel matrix. Based on this compact IOR, a coarse TF domain CE is first performed using a linear minimum mean square error estimator. Then, the resultant TF domain estimates are transformed to the DD domain through a unitary transformation for further refinement. We reveal that the effective DD domain channel matrix can be viewed as an aggregation of multiple DD domain channel responses with different phase shifts. This allows us to devise a threshold-based estimation for DD domain channel parameters with high accuracy. The estimated DD domain channel parameters are then applied to form a refined estimate of TF domain channel. Our numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method can achieve substantial performance gains over conventional OFDM channel estimation techniques under the same pilot deployment.

</details>


### [208] [Learning-Aided Short Code Design for ISAC based on MIMO-OFDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09797)
*Mingcheng Nie,Shuangyang Li,Geng Wang,Peng Cheng,Shenghong Li,Chang Liu,Giuseppe Caire,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的短码长ISAC编码波形设计方法，利用Transformer发射机和接收机直接联合优化通信误码率与感知测距精度，在实际MIMO-OFDM架构下实现通信可靠性与测距性能之间的灵活权衡，并优于传统信道编码与调制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在短码长传输场景中，通信可靠性与感知测距精度通常存在明显冲突，而传统基于独立信道编码和调制的设计难以灵活刻画和优化二者之间的权衡。因此，论文旨在构建一种能够面向ISAC任务联合学习编码波形的实用方法。

Method: 论文采用实际的MIMO-OFDM系统架构，并假设发射端已知通信信道状态信息以及静态目标角度信息。基于Transformer构建发射机，将输入信息比特直接编码为ISAC发射波形，同时联合优化通信BER和时延修正Cramer-Rao界（MCRB）。在通信接收端，采用对应的Transformer接收机恢复信息比特。此外，通过分析学习得到的码字，比较通信导向、感知导向和均衡ISAC波形的结构特征。

Result: 数值结果展示了不同任务导向下学习码字的结构，并表明均衡型ISAC波形在结构上自然处于通信导向和感知导向设计之间。与采用标准信道编码和调制的传统方案相比，所提方法在通信与测距性能权衡方面取得了显著增益。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，基于Transformer的端到端深度学习编码能够有效适配短码长MIMO-OFDM ISAC系统，并以统一方式实现通信与感知性能的联合优化。学习到的波形具有可解释的任务导向结构，说明该方法具备灵活调节ISAC性能权衡的潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a deep learning (DL)-based coded waveform design for integrated sensing and communications (ISAC), enabling flexible trade-offs between communication reliability and ranging accuracy in short-block transmissions. The proposed scheme is built upon a practical multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (MIMO-OFDM) architecture, where the communication channel state information and the angles of the static targets are assumed available at the transmitter. A transformer-based transmitter encodes input information bits directly into ISAC transmit waveforms to jointly optimize the bit error rate (BER) performance and the delay modified Cramer-Rao bound (MCRB). A corresponding transformer-based receiver is adopted at the communication side to recover the transmitted information bits. We further examine the learned codewords for communication-oriented and sensing-oriented designs, revealing that a balanced ISAC waveform naturally exhibits an intermediate structure between these two extremes. Numerical results illustrate these codeword structures and demonstrate that the proposed design provides substantial trade-off gains over conventional schemes based on standard channel coding and modulation.

</details>


### [209] [Proximity Gaps for Gabidulin Codes and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09838)
*Songsong Li,Chaoping Xing,Chen Yuan,Ruiqi Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究线性秩度量码的 proximity gap，并将结果应用于交错 Gabidulin 码的 IOPP 和 q-线性化多项式承诺方案（PCS）。作者证明一般线性秩度量码具有 (d-1)/(3n) 的 proximity gap，Gabidulin 码可提升至 (d-1)/(2n)，且后者渐近意义下紧；进一步构造了首个基于秩度量纠错码的 PCS 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Proximity gap 是交互式邻近性预言证明（IOPP）和多项式承诺方案（PCS）正确性的核心。汉明度量码的 proximity gap 已较为成熟，但秩度量码的对应理论仍不充分，而后者在编码理论和密码学中具有重要应用。因此，本文旨在刻画线性秩度量码，尤其是 Gabidulin 码的 proximity gap，并建立其密码学应用。

Method: 作者首先分析仿射直线 ℓ_{u0,u1} 上各点到线性秩度量码 C 的距离分布：证明任意 [n,k,d] 线性秩度量码在 δ≤(d−1)/(3n) 时具有 proximity gap，错误率至多为 q^{e+1}/q^m，其中 e=floor(δn)。随后利用 Gabidulin 码的特殊结构，将阈值提升至 (d−1)/(2n)，错误率控制为 10q^{n−1}/q^m。作者还构造常率 Gabidulin 码与相应仿射直线，证明 (d−1)/(2n) 的界是紧的；并在 d/(3n) 处给出错误率下界反例。最后，将 Ligero IOPP 从交错 Reed–Solomon 码适配到交错 Gabidulin 码，再将基于 Ligero 的普通多项式 PCS 改造为 q-线性化多项式 PCS。

Result: 一般线性秩度量码获得 δ≤(d−1)/(3n)、错误率至多 q^{e+1}/q^m 的 proximity gap；Gabidulin 码获得更强的 δ≤(d−1)/(2n)、错误率至多 10q^{n−1}/q^m 的 gap。作者证明后一个阈值是紧的：存在常率 Gabidulin 码和仿射直线，使得 1−o(1) 比例的点距离码至多 d/(2n)，但方向向量 u1 至少距离码 3d/(4n)。此外，作者给出 d/(3n) 阈值下错误率的下界，并构造交错 Gabidulin 码的 IOPP 以及 q-线性化多项式 PCS。

Conclusion: 本文建立了线性秩度量码 proximity gap 的基础理论，显示一般码和 Gabidulin 码分别达到与一般线性汉明度量码和 Reed–Solomon 码相对应的界限。Gabidulin 码的 (d−1)/(2n) 界被证明是紧的。基于这些结果，本文进一步实现了交错 Gabidulin 码的 IOPP，并提出据作者所知首个基于秩度量纠错码的 PCS 框架，拓展了秩度量码在密码学协议中的应用。

Abstract: Proximity gaps are central to the soundness of interactive oracle proofs of proximity (IOPPs) and polynomial commitment schemes (PCSs). An $[n,k,d]$ linear code $C\subseteq\mathbb F^n$ has a $δ$-proximity gap with error $ε$ if, for every $u_0,u_1\in\mathbb F^n$, either all points on $\ell_{u_0,u_1}=\{u_0+αu_1:α\in\mathbb F\}$ are $δ$-close to $C$, or at most an $ε$ fraction are. Although proximity gaps for Hamming-metric codes are well understood, their rank-metric counterparts remain largely unexplored despite their applications in coding theory and cryptography. In this work, we study proximity gaps for linear rank-metric codes and their cryptographic applications.
  First, we show that every $[n,k,d]$ linear rank-metric code $C$ over $\mathbb F_{q^m}$ admits a proximity gap for every $δ\le(d-1)/(3n)$, with error at most $q^{e+1}/q^m$, where $e=\lfloorδn\rfloor$. For Gabidulin codes, we improve the gap to $(d-1)/(2n)$ with error $10q^{n-1}/q^m$. These two proximity gaps match those for general linear Hamming-metric codes and Reed--Solomon (RS) codes, respectively. We prove the $(d-1)/(2n)$ bound is tight by constructing an infinite family of constant-rate Gabidulin codes and affine lines $\ell_{u_0,u_1}$ on which a $1-o(1)$ fraction of points are $d/(2n)$-close to the code, while $u_1$ is at least $3d/(4n)$-far from it. At the $d/(3n)$ gap, we also give a counterexample establishing a lower bound on $ε$.
  As applications, we construct an IOPP for interleaved Gabidulin codes by adapting the Ligero IOPP for interleaved RS codes. We then adapt the Ligero-based PCS for ordinary polynomials to obtain a $q$-linearized polynomial commitment scheme. To our knowledge, this is the first PCS framework based on rank-metric error-correcting codes.

</details>


### [210] [Fast Collision-Free Acquisition in 1-Persistent Age-Threshold Slotted ALOHA via Role-Protected Counters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09934)
*Plínio Santini Dester*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出RP-CTSA协议，通过将预约记忆与信息年龄AoI分离，改善1-pTSA在有限时域内的获取瞬态性能。协议利用角色保护和计数器阈值机制，在无中心调度和无需识别碰撞节点的条件下，实现碰撞后更稳定的时隙预约。理论上，当阈值Γ_n=n时获取时间为O(n²)，当Γ_n∼(1+θ)n（θ>0）时可降为O(n log n)；仿真显示其有限时域AoI显著优于1-pTSA，同时保留所有无碰撞调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向目标的感知、估计与控制依赖及时且持续的任务相关更新。虽然1-pTSA能够自组织形成周期性无碰撞调度，但其初始获取阶段可能耗时较长，并在有限观测时域内主导整体性能，导致AoI较高。因此，论文旨在设计一种无需中心化相位分配、又能缩短获取瞬态并降低有限时域AoI的随机接入协议。

Method: 提出角色保护计数器阈值时隙ALOHA（RP-CTSA）。核心思想是将预约状态记忆与AoI分离：已调度且单独发送成功的节点即使与活跃竞争者发生碰撞，仍保留原有相位；若多个已调度节点彼此发生碰撞，则立即释放其预约角色。AoI仅在更新被成功解码后重置。协议依赖单节点确认以及二值RELEASE/HOLD反馈，不需要中心化相位分配，也不需要识别具体碰撞节点。在逆人口规模接入条件下，将获取过程精确表示为纯死亡过程，并据此分析不同计数器阈值Γ_n下的获取时间复杂度。

Result: 理论结果表明：当Γ_n=n时，获取时间为O(n²)；当Γ_n∼(1+θ)n且θ>0时，获取时间改善为O(n log n)。仿真验证了这两种渐近标度关系，并显示RP-CTSA在有限时域内相较1-pTSA具有显著AoI收益。同时，该协议能够保留所有无碰撞调度。

Conclusion: RP-CTSA通过保护已建立的调度角色、仅在必要时释放冲突角色，有效降低了预约获取阶段对有限时域性能的负面影响。其在分布式、无碰撞识别和无中心协调条件下实现了更快的获取速度与更低的AoI，尤其在阈值略高于节点数时可达到O(n log n)的获取复杂度，体现出较好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Goal-oriented sensing, estimation, and control benefit from prompt, regular access to task-relevant updates. Although 1-persistent age-threshold slotted ALOHA (1-pTSA) can self-organize into a periodic collision-free schedule, its acquisition transient can dominate finite-horizon performance. We propose role-protected counter-threshold slotted ALOHA (RP--CTSA), which separates reservation memory from the age of information (AoI). A scheduled singleton retains its phase after colliding with active contenders, whereas a collision involving multiple scheduled nodes releases them immediately; AoI resets only after a decoded update. The protocol requires individual acknowledgments and a binary RELEASE/HOLD indication, but no centralized phase assignment or identification of colliders. Let $n$ denote the number of nodes and $Γ_n$ the counter threshold. Under inverse-population access scaling, the acquisition dynamics admit an exact pure-death representation. When $Γ_n=n$, the acquisition time is $O(n^2)$; when $Γ_n\sim(1+θ)n$, with $θ>0$, it is $O(n\log n)$. Simulations confirm both regimes and show substantial finite-horizon AoI gains over 1-pTSA while preserving every collision-free schedule.

</details>


### [211] [Quantum MDS codes from complements of unions of finite-field subsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09943)
*Naihong Hu,Hong Ji*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文针对奇素数幂 q，利用有限域 F_{q^2} 中若干集合的补集构造定位集，建立广义 Reed–Solomon 码满足 Hermitian 自正交的充分条件，并由此得到五族 q 元量子 MDS 码。部分参数下最小距离超过 q/2+1，且相较多种已有构造可获得严格改进，并且这种改进对无穷多个 q 成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于迹映射、线性变换及乘法子群陪集的量子 MDS 码构造在部分参数范围内存在最小距离限制。论文旨在通过新的定位集设计和 Hermitian 自正交 GRS 码构造，扩大量子 MDS 码的参数范围，并在相同码长下提升最小距离。

Method: 将 F_{q^2} 中若干子集的并集取补集作为 GRS 码定位集；先建立一个保证 GRS 码 Hermitian 自正交的充分条件，再结合乘法子群陪集以及具有预先指定迹值或范数值的集合，构造五族 Hermitian 自正交 GRS 码。随后利用 Hermitian 构造将这些经典码转换为 q 元量子 MDS 码，并通过参数比较分析其与已有构造及传播规则所得码的性能差异。

Result: 获得五族 Hermitian 自正交 GRS 码及相应的五族 q 元量子 MDS 码。在适当参数条件下，所得量子码的最小距离大于 q/2+1；对相同码长进行比较时，论文给出了新构造严格优于若干基于迹映射、线性变换和乘法子群陪集的已有构造的条件，并证明这种改进对无穷多个 q 成立。

Conclusion: 通过补集型定位集与迹、范数结构的结合，论文有效拓展了量子 MDS 码的构造方法和参数范围。其主要贡献是提供了五类具有较大最小距离的 q 元量子 MDS 码，并在无限多个有限域规模上证明了相对于已有方法的严格性能优势。

Abstract: Let $q$ be an odd prime power. We use complements of unions of subsets of $\mathbb F_{q^2}$ as locator sets and establish a sufficient condition under which a generalized Reed--Solomon (GRS) code is Hermitian self-orthogonal. Using cosets of multiplicative subgroups and sets with prescribed trace or norm values, we construct five families of Hermitian self-orthogonal GRS codes over $\mathbb F_{q^2}$. The Hermitian construction then yields five corresponding families of $q$-ary quantum maximum-distance-separable (MDS) codes. Under suitable parameter conditions, these quantum codes have minimum distances greater than $q/2+1$. By comparing codes of the same length, we give conditions under which our codes have strictly larger minimum distances than those obtainable from several previously known constructions based on trace maps, linear transformations, and cosets of multiplicative subgroups, either directly or via the propagation rule. We further show that such improvements occur for infinitely many values of $q$.

</details>


### [212] [The kernel-block rank profile and a complete classification of $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-linear Hadamard codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09969)
*Dipak K. Bhunia*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究Z2Z4Z8-加性Hadamard码的等价分类问题，提出核块秩剖面这一新的等价不变量，并证明它能够区分此前长度、秩和核维数相同但类型不同的码。该不变量可恢复参数t1，再结合长度和核维数恢复t2、t3，从而完全刻画同长度码的等价关系；长度为2^t时，非等价码的数量为floor((t^2+6)/12)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Z2Z4Z8-线性Hadamard码族具有已知的长度、线性、秩和核维数等参数，但这些全局不变量无法完全区分不同类型的码。存在两类无限参数族具有相同长度、秩和核维数；对于部分长度，过去只能依靠计算机等价性检验进行区分。因此，需要一种可计算、具有理论解释且能够完全区分这些码的新等价不变量。

Method: 作者利用码的核对二进制坐标进行分块：若任意核字在两个坐标上的取值始终相同，则将这两个坐标归入同一块。随后定义核块秩剖面，即在各坐标块上 puncture（删去其他坐标）后所得线性张成空间维数的多重集。作者对整个参数族计算该剖面，证明共有2^(t1+t2+t3-1)个块，每块大小为2^(2t1+t2)，剖面值至多为t2+binom(t1+2,2)和t2+2+binom(t1+1,2)两种。两者之差为t1-1，因此剖面能够确定t1；再结合长度关系t+1=3t1+2t2+t3及核维数确定其余参数。

Result: 核块秩剖面在t1>1时通常呈现两个不同值，在且仅在t1=1时为常值。该剖面能够恢复参数t1，而长度与核维数能够恢复t2和t3。因此，同一长度下，族中的两个码等价当且仅当它们的类型相同。长度为2^t、t≥3时，成对非等价码的数量为floor((t^2+6)/12)。

Conclusion: 本文解决了该Z2Z4Z8-线性Hadamard码族中由传统全局参数无法区分的等价分类问题。核块秩剖面提供了有效的局部结构不变量，不仅替代了部分计算机等价性测试，还证明了该参数族的类型完全决定同长度码的等价类。研究的核心贡献是将核结构与局部秩信息结合，实现了完整分类。

Abstract: The $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-additive codes are subgroups of $\mathbb{Z}_2^{α_1}\times\mathbb{Z}_4^{α_2}\times\mathbb{Z}_8^{α_3}$, and a $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-linear Hadamard code is the Gray map image of such a code. A recursive construction of $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-additive Hadamard codes $\mathcal H^{t_1,t_2,t_3}$, with all $α_i\neq0$, $t_1\geq1$, $t_2\geq0$, and $t_3\geq1$, is known, as are the linearity, kernel dimension, and rank of the corresponding codes $H^{t_1,t_2,t_3}$ of length $2^t$, where $t+1=3t_1+2t_2+t_3$. Yet these invariants do not completely classify the family. Two infinite families of pairs of distinct types share the length, rank and kernel dimension, and for classified lengths $2^t$, $3\leq t\leq11$, such pairs were separated only by computer equivalence tests.
  In this paper, we introduce an equivalence invariant that resolves these cases. The kernel partitions the binary coordinates into blocks, two coordinates lying in the same block when every kernel word takes the same value in both; the \emph{kernel-block rank profile} is the multiset of the dimensions of the linear span punctured on these blocks. Unlike rank and kernel dimension, which are global, this invariant records how much of the span survives on each block. We compute it for the whole family: there are $2^{t_1+t_2+t_3-1}$ blocks, all of size $2^{2t_1+t_2}$, and the profile takes at most two values $t_2+\binom{t_1+2}{2}$ and $t_2+2+\binom{t_1+1}{2}$, whose difference is $t_1-1$; it is constant precisely when $t_1=1$. Hence, the profile recovers $t_1$, and the length and kernel dimension recover $t_2$ and $t_3$. Two codes of the family with the same length are therefore equivalent if and only if their types coincide, and the number of pairwise nonequivalent such codes of length $2^t$ is $\lfloor(t^2+6)/12\rfloor$ for every $t\geq3$.

</details>


### [213] [On the Evolution of the Capacity-Achieving Input Support for the Amplitude-Constrained AWGN Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10039)
*Luca Barletta,Alex Dytso*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究峰值幅度约束下 AWGN 信道容量达到分布的支撑集如何随幅度约束 A 变化。核心结论是：非零支撑点局部稳定，支撑基数的局部变化只能发生在原点，具体表现为原点的出现、消失，或原点分裂/合并为对称非零点对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在峰值幅度约束的加性白高斯噪声信道中，容量达到输入分布具有唯一性、对称性、离散性和有限支撑等已知性质，但其支撑集随幅度约束变化时如何发生跃迁仍缺乏完整刻画。特别是哪些支撑点能够产生或消失、支撑基数如何局部改变，是理解最优输入结构的重要问题。

Method: 作者分析容量达到分布对应的 KKT 函数及其接触点结构。首先证明原点是唯一可能的退化支撑点，也是唯一可能的非活跃接触点；随后建立最优分布和 KKT 函数关于幅度约束的连续性。利用这些连续性结果，对每个非零支撑点进行局部稳定性分析，证明其在幅度约束小幅变化时会持续为唯一的邻近原子，且位置与概率质量连续变化。最后结合对称性、接触点性质和局部稳定性，刻画支撑基数的可能变化形式。

Result: 证明了每个非零支撑点在局部扰动下都稳定存在，因此邻近幅度下支撑基数只能保持不变或增加一个。任何局部增加都只能发生在原点。由此，支撑基数的局部变化仅限于原点：原点出现或消失，或者原点分裂为一对对称非零支撑点；反向过程则对应这对支撑点合并为原点。

Conclusion: 本文将 CAID 支撑集的局部跃迁机制严格限制在原点附近，排除了非零支撑点发生局部生成、消失或任意重组的可能性。这为理解峰值幅度变化下最优输入分布的结构演化提供了清晰的局部理论框架。

Abstract: We consider an additive white Gaussian noise channel subject to a peak-amplitude constraint and study how the support of the capacity-achieving input distribution (CAID) evolves as the amplitude constraint $A$ varies. Although the CAID is known to be unique, symmetric, discrete, and finitely supported, the structure of its support transitions has remained largely unresolved. We show that the origin is the only possible degenerate support point and the only possible inactive contact point of the KKT function. We then establish continuity properties of the optimal distribution and the KKT function and prove that every nonzero support point is locally stable: under small changes in $A$, it persists as a unique nearby atom whose location and probability mass vary continuously. Combining these results, we prove that, locally, the support cardinality at a nearby amplitude is either unchanged or larger by one and that any such increase can occur only at the origin. Consequently, all local changes in support cardinality are confined to the origin: locally, the only possible transition mechanisms are the appearance or disappearance of the point at the origin and the splitting or merging of the origin into a symmetric pair.

</details>


### [214] [Frame-Coded Legged Locomotion over Noisy Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10273)
*Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将粗糙地形下的多足机器人开环运动建模为带擦除与噪声的有限帧模拟编码问题：身体级命令被冗余映射为异质局部接触命令，存活接触通过柔顺形态实现加权解码。论文建立了重构阈值、可靠性指数、噪声放大、刚度裕度及增量冗余规则，提出多足运动的信息论基本极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多足运动的“重复传输”解释把每条腿视为执行相同标量任务，虽能利用空间冗余抵抗接触丢失，却缺乏正的任务维度速率，也没有随存活接触集合变化的自适应解码器。本文旨在建立一个能处理多维身体命令、接触擦除/扰动以及机械柔顺解码的统一理论，并刻画冗余与可靠性的基本极限。

Method: 将d维身体级命令编码为N>d个异质局部接触命令，采用有限帧与擦除信道模型描述粗糙地形。利用接触门控的柔顺形态实现加权主动子帧解码，并在线性高斯模型中证明：机械平衡等于后验均值，切向刚度等于后验精度，机械柔顺性等于后验协方差。进一步分析等范数Parseval帧的单接触鲁棒最优性、帧相干性对双接触鲁棒性的影响，并构造可实现的谐波帧步态族；对独立存活概率q的随机高斯帧，推导模拟维度速率、可靠性指数、噪声放大和增量冗余分配规则。

Result: 等范数Parseval帧对一次接触缺失具有极小极大最优性，双接触鲁棒性由帧相干性控制。随机高斯步态帧在任意速率R<q时可实现精确重构，并具有二项式可靠性指数；当R>q时，任意命令恢复不可能。接触噪声使每个模态的渐近放大倍数为1/(q-R)，且接近阈值时机械刚度裕度趋于零。信息—运动不等式和精确增量冗余规则要求将下一步态分量分配给最柔软且与任务相关的未解析模态。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一个将多足粗糙地形运动统一为模拟帧编码与机械解码的理论框架，给出了有限相对冗余下的可实现重构条件及其反演界限。结果表明，接触冗余不仅能提高运动鲁棒性，还可通过帧设计和柔顺机械实现转化为可量化的信息传输能力；阈值q是任务速率、噪声放大和机械刚度退化的共同临界点。

Abstract: Open-loop multilegged locomotion over rough terrain has been interpreted as matter transport over a noisy channel: leg-ground interactions are discrete basic active contacts, terrain deletes or perturbs those contacts, and spatial redundancy concentrates the resulting thrust and arrival time. That construction is repetition-like because every module carries the same scalar locomotion task. It consequently provides neither a positive task rate nor a decoder that changes with the surviving contact set. Here we formulate locomotion instead as a quantized finite-frame expansion with erasures. A d-dimensional body-level command is mapped into N>d heterogeneous local contact commands. Rough terrain erases or corrupts frame coefficients, while a contact-gated compliant morphology physically realizes the weighted active-subframe decoder. For a linear-Gaussian model, mechanical equilibrium is exactly the posterior mean, tangent stiffness is posterior precision, and mechanical compliance is posterior covariance. Equal-norm Parseval frames are shown to be minimax optimal against one missing contact, two-contact robustness is governed by frame coherence, and a harmonic frame gives a directly realizable gait family. For independently surviving contacts of probability q, random Gaussian gait frames admit exact reconstruction at every analog dimension rate R<q, with a binomial reliability exponent, whereas recovery of arbitrary commands is impossible for R>q. Residual contact noise yields an asymptotic per-mode amplification 1/(q-R) and a vanishing mechanical stiffness margin at the threshold. An information-locomotion inequality and an exact incremental-redundancy rule direct the next gait component toward the softest task-relevant unresolved mode. The resulting analog frame-coding theorem establishes a finite relative redundancy and converse as part of a fundamental limit theory of legged locomotion.

</details>


### [215] [Revisiting the Weight Spectrum of the Affine Grassmann Code $C^{\mathbb{A}}(2,m)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10274)
*Rohit Yadav*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究仿射Grassmann码 C^{A}(2,m) 的汉明重量谱，提出一种适用于所有 m≥4 和任意有限域 F_q 的统一、简洁推导方法。核心是将码字表示为紧凑的矩阵形式，并把其汉明重量写成由两个显式仿射子空间及其交集决定的单一闭式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于 C^{A}(2,m) 重量谱的结果依赖于对相关交错矩阵秩的分类讨论，分析较为繁琐。本文旨在给出一种独立且更 streamlined（更简洁、更系统）的推导，减少分类情形，并获得对所有参数范围统一有效的表达。

Method: 将码字统一写成紧凑的矩阵形式；随后通过矩阵所定义的结构识别两个显式仿射子空间，并利用这两个子空间及其交集的大小来计算码字的汉明重量。该方法绕开或弱化了单纯按交错矩阵秩进行的大量分情况讨论。

Result: 得到适用于所有 m≥4 及任意素数幂 q 的统一重量表达式：码字汉明重量可由两个显式仿射子空间的规模及其交集，通过一个单一闭式公式计算。该结果提供了对既有重量谱结论的独立推导。

Conclusion: 本文证明了仿射Grassmann码 C^{A}(2,m) 的重量谱可以用一种更紧凑、统一的矩阵—仿射子空间框架刻画。相较已有基于秩分类的方法，该框架推导更简洁，并在参数范围上具有完全统一性。

Abstract: Affine Grassmann codes, introduced by Beelen, Ghorpade and Høholdt, are linear codes over $\mathbb{F}_q$ obtained by evaluating linear combinations of minors of a generic matrix. The weight spectrum of the affine Grassmann code $C^{\mathbb{A}}(2,m)$ was determined by Piñero and Singh via a case analysis on the rank of an associated alternating matrix. We give an independent and more streamlined derivation, valid for all $m\ge4$ and every prime power $q$, in which each codeword is written in a compact matrix form and its Hamming weight is expressed through a single closed formula involving two explicit affine subspaces and their intersection.

</details>


### [216] [Construction of Multi-sequences With High Nonlinear Complexity via Narrow Ray Class Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10369)
*Xiaofeng Liu,Jun Zhang,Fang-Wei Fu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一构造框架：结合窄射线类域与 Guruswami–Xing 的循环下降方法，在任意亏格的函数域上构造具有高非线性复杂度的多序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性复杂度是衡量伪随机序列质量的重要指标，而在密码学中构造具有高非线性复杂度的多序列具有理论与实际价值。已有研究分别给出了相关高复杂度多序列构造，但缺少更统一、适用范围更广的框架。

Method: 论文利用窄射线类域（narrow ray class fields）和 Guruswami–Xing 提出的循环下降（cyclic descent）方法，建立统一的多序列构造框架，并将其应用于函数域。该框架能够处理具有任意亏格的函数域。

Result: 研究者由此生成了一类新的多序列，并证明这些序列具有较高的非线性复杂度。构造结果不再局限于特定亏格，适用范围扩展到任意亏格的函数域。

Conclusion: 本文统一并推广了此前关于高非线性复杂度多序列的构造方法，说明窄射线类域与循环下降技术能够有效产生密码学上有价值的伪随机多序列。

Abstract: Nonlinear complexity is a fundamental criterion in the evaluation of pseudorandom sequences. The construction of multi-sequences with high nonlinear complexity is both theoretically and practically important in cryptography. Motivated by prior constructions of multi-sequences with high nonlinear complexity in [IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory, 60(10), 2014] and [IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory, 63(12), 2017], we provide a unified framework via narrow ray class fields, the cyclic descent due to Guruswami and Xing in [J. Combin. Theory Ser. A 129 (2015) ]. Then we can generate new multi-sequences with high nonlinear complexity over function fields with arbitrary genera.

</details>


### [217] [Mobility Information Capacity in the Sky: A Gaussian Channel Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10436)
*Weijie Yuan,Fan Liu,Shuangyang Li,Lin Zhou,Pingzhi Fan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出“机动信息容量”作为低空无线网络中的信息论度量，用于衡量在给定机动资源预算和环境不确定性下，轨迹观测能够揭示的有意机动输入的最大信息量。论文建立线性高斯机动信道，推导有限时域容量，并进一步得到基于水填充的最优资源分配和类香农渐近容量定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空域容量指标主要关注飞行器占用数量或交通流量，但相同数量的飞行器可能因运动选择不同而表现出显著不同的机动复杂度与可辨识性。因此，论文旨在建立一种以运动信息和轨迹可辨识性为核心的容量度量，补充传统占用型或流量型指标。

Method: 针对固定反馈架构，构造提升形式的线性高斯机动信道，将有意机动输入、轨迹观测、机动资源约束及环境不确定性纳入统一信息论模型。通过代价白化和不确定性白化提取时空机动特征模态，利用有限时域对数行列式公式计算容量，并采用水填充策略实现机动资源在不同模态间的最优分配。在非退化模态数随时间线性增长且模态效率渐近对称的条件下，进一步推导类香农容量定律。

Result: 论文得到有限时域的机动信息容量表达式，并识别出由时空机动特征决定的机动特征模态。最优资源分配遵循水填充规律。渐近条件下，容量可写为 R_M^G=(B_M/2)log_2(1+MNR)，其中MNR为机动噪声比，B_M表示相应的有效机动带宽或模态增长率。该结果表明，容量随机动资源与环境噪声之比呈对数增长。

Conclusion: 本文建立了面向低空空域的运动中心型容量分析框架，将轨迹对有意机动的可辨识程度纳入容量定义。该指标可作为传统空域容量研究的补充，并提供清晰的模态分解和资源分配解释。但作者明确指出，它是区分性基线，尚未纳入碰撞约束、几何可行域或安全间隔等因素，因此不能直接等同于工程意义上的空域承载容量。

Abstract: Existing airspace capacity metrics mainly quantify occupancy or flow, although the same number of aerial vehicles may result in different motion alternatives. This letter establishes \emph{mobility information capacity} as an information-theoretic measure for low-altitude wireless networks. It quantifies the maximum information that trajectory observations reveal about intentional maneuver inputs under a given maneuver-resource budget and environmental uncertainty. For a common fixed feedback architecture, we formulate a lifted linear-Gaussian mobility channel and derive its finite-horizon log-determinant capacity. Cost and uncertainty whitening gives the spatiotemporal mobility eigenmodes, whose optimal maneuver-resource allocation follows water-filling. When the number of nondegenerate modes grows linearly with time and their efficiencies become asymptotically symmetric, we arrive at the Shannon-like law $R_M^{\rm G}=\frac{B_M}{2}\log_2(1+\mathrm{MNR})$, where MNR is the mobility-to-noise ratio. The proposed measure opens a motion-centric capacity perspective for the sky, while remaining a distinguishability baseline rather than a collision- or geometry-constrained airspace capacity.

</details>


### [218] [Risk-Averse Decision Making via Quantum Measurement Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10482)
*Meiyi Zhu,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究风险厌恶量子测量设计：以优化确定性等价（OCE）替代单纯平均效用，并将优化问题化为有限个半定规划；在二态判别中得到具有Helstrom测量形式的闭式解，数值结果表明其可改善效用分布的低尾风险，但仅付出适度平均效用代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子测量通常最大化平均效用，但当测量结果将作为更大决策系统中的行动时，平均值无法充分反映低效用或糟糕结果的风险。因此，需要一种能够控制下尾风险、兼顾平均表现的风险厌恶量子测量设计准则。

Method: 采用优化确定性等价（OCE）作为风险厌恶目标函数，该框架涵盖平均效用和条件风险价值（CVaR）等准则。针对分段线性增益函数，将测量优化问题转化为有限个半定规划，并推导其对偶形式；对于两个量子态的判别问题，进一步推导闭式最优解。

Result: 理论上，分段线性增益下的OCE优化可通过有限个半定规划求解，并具有相应的对偶表述。二态判别问题的最优测量具有Helstrom测量的形式。数值实验显示，风险优化测量能够改善效用分布的低尾部分，同时平均效用仅有中等程度的下降。

Conclusion: 研究表明，量子测量设计不必局限于最大化平均效用；基于OCE的风险厌恶优化能够在平均性能与尾部风险之间实现可控权衡，并为风险敏感型量子决策提供了可计算的优化框架。

Abstract: Quantum measurements are conventionally optimized to maximize the average of a utility that depends on the true state and on the measurement outcome. However, when the outcome of the measurement is used as an action within a larger decision-making system, the average utility does not capture the risk of poor outcomes. This letter addresses the design of quantum measurements that maximize a risk-averse objective given by the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), a family of criteria that includes the average utility and the conditional value at risk (CVaR) as special cases. For a piecewise linear gain function, defining the OCE, thus including the CVaR, the problem is shown to reduce to a finite number of semidefinite programs, for which a dual formulation is derived. For the discrimination of two states, a closed-form solution is obtained that takes the form of a Helstrom measurement. Numerical results show that the optimized measurement improves the lower tail of the utility distribution at a moderate cost in average utility.

</details>
